To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [113] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过监测通用模型与领域专家模型间的Jensen-Shannon散度，在专家模型出现推理风险时动态切换至通用模型，实现两者能力融合，在科学基准上超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大模型擅长推理但缺乏领域知识，领域专家模型知识丰富但存在逻辑减弱和鲁棒性下降等副作用，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者优势。

Method: 借鉴投机解码的draft-and-verify思想，将其重构为自适应路由机制：在每个token计算专家与通用模型的Jensen-Shannon散度，当散度显著时判定为推理风险，即时将控制权交给通用模型，实现推理时策略组合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench基准测试，Divergence Decoding性能优于领域专用模型和通用模型，且超过多数单模型基线。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用且无需训练的推理时协作范式，可有效融合不同LLM的能力，为模型能力组合开辟新途径。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [114] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出AnchorMark，一种无需训练、基于反演的鲁棒水印方法，利用潜在空间的旋转同步性，通过中心区域锚点估计并校正旋转角度，以抵抗含旋转的复合攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反演的水印在遭遇旋转等复合有损后处理时，因空间对应关系被破坏而导致解码失败，亟需一种无需训练且能处理旋转的鲁棒方案。

Method: 发现图像域旋转与恢复初始潜变量旋转角度一致的“旋转同步”特性，据此在初始潜变量中心区域嵌入同步锚点，提取时估计旋转角并校正。

Result: 在旋转及组合攻击下显著提升比特准确率，同时对图像质量影响有限。

Conclusion: AnchorMark有效增强水印对旋转攻击的鲁棒性，为反演水印提供了实用且高效的新路径。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [115] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，包含增量直接推导溯因模块PIE-Abducer和时序知识图谱溯因规划模块PIE-APT，在描述逻辑（DL）上原生建模动态知识图谱的非单调状态转移，通过直接反驳推导提取缺失前提，避免最小碰集（MHS）组合搜索，并采用递归生成-测试架构在有限因果深度内结合A*反向链和正向时序投影验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱在开放世界环境下存在信息不完整问题，传统动作形式化面临可判定性难题和Ramification问题，而基于结构溯因的MHS枚举存在组合爆炸。现有方法难以在表达性、可判定性和完备性之间取得平衡，亟需一种无需外部模态算子、原生支持DL且避免MHS的组合方法。

Method: 框架包含两模块：PIE-Abducer通过将目标目标的逻辑否定注入一致分支，利用直接反驳后果提取缺失前提，绕过MHS枚举；PIE-APT采用递归生成-测试架构，将反向链A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交织至有界因果深度，再通过正向链时序投影严格验证。整个状态转移建模为对演绎封闭DL理论的非单调更新，以增量推理器为黑盒，保持逻辑可判定性。

Result: 在四个OWL基准上测试参数化目标搜索、推理中DL蕴含、开放世界假设注入和对抗性矛盾发现等能力，结果显示经典规划器在语义能力上被超越，且直接推导方法在溯因丰富度上定量优于MHS基线。

Conclusion: 所提框架在保留DL高表达性的同时保证了可判定性，并通过避免组合搜索实现了高效且鲁棒的不完整知识管理，为动态知识图谱上的规划与推理提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [116] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 针对交通预测中基于图的AI模型，本文指出现有鲁棒性评估因不切实际的威胁模型和非目标攻击而失真，提出一种仅操控少量传感器的物理感知目标攻击，并将鲁棒性重构为检测问题，引入物理信息检测器与防御性预测器组合，在多种模型和数据集上优于对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性评估采用无界扰动和非目标攻击，未能反映实际场景；实际攻击可局部化到特定路段，导致错误到达时间或绕行，而对抗训练在物理感知攻击下迁移性差。

Method: 设计物理感知攻击模拟真实拥堵，仅监控和操纵少数传感器以倍增目标链路误差；提出将物理信息检测器输出作为特征输入冻结的预测器，并针对自适应攻击进行联合训练。

Result: 物理攻击使目标链路误差倍增而全网误差几乎不变，对抗训练难以抵抗；检测-缓解防御在15个模型-数据集设置中的13个上优于针对该攻击的对抗训练，对留出攻击提升最大，清洁数据成本近乎零。

Conclusion: 强调评估AI对抗攻击需结合应用特定约束，将鲁棒性视为检测问题比依赖扰动限定的对抗训练更有效。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [117] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SubCASP方法，基于隐马尔可夫模型融合IDS日志，推断变电站网络攻击的当前阶段、下一阶段及历史路径，在可复现攻击图数据集上验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDS仅报告孤立症状，缺乏攻击阶段上下文，难以及时部署缓解措施。

Method: 利用ATT&CK威胁建模定义攻击阶段，采用HMM融合多个IDS数据日志，推断当前阶段、预测下一步并回溯攻击路径。

Result: 在不同IDS可观测性和缺失日志场景下，SubCASP表现稳健，测试结果证明其有效性。

Conclusion: SubCASP能提供攻击阶段上下文，支持针对IEC 61850变电站多阶段网络攻击的缓解决策。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型在对抗梯度攻击下表现稳健，但对自然噪声无内在防御，且普遍过度自信；行为脆弱性源于解码器路由失败而非输入编码问题，提示表面补丁无效，需将鲁棒性融入迭代解码过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归模型的替代方案，具备双向上下文和迭代精炼优势，但其在自然噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未充分探索，现有研究缺乏系统性的架构内在属性与权重依赖行为的区分。

Method: 采用参数匹配的模型对（LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B 和 Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B），覆盖32种自然扰动条件、对抗梯度探测及机制性隐状态分析，通过配对设计隔离架构与权重的影响。

Result: 发现DLM对基于梯度的对抗后缀自然抵抗，但对自然噪声无保障；存在系统性过度自信；机制探测显示所有模型能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全归因于解码器路由失败，且表面提示修补无效。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性不能通过表面修补实现，必须从根本上集成到迭代解码循环中，为未来鲁棒扩散模型设计提供关键方向。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [119] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CHARGE框架，利用CWE层次结构和LLM为未验证RTL模块自动生成安全属性，在多个SoC设计上检测出已知和新增漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件安全属性生成依赖可信设计规范且人工成本高，缺乏自动化方法，需利用CWE和LLM减少人工干预并提高准确性。

Method: 利用CWE条目的层次结构推理，识别安全关键资产，推断预期安全行为，并从资产和CWE语义生成属性，无需可信规范。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18/19/21上检测42个已知漏洞中的27个；OpenPiton上89%的SVA可运行且92.2%非空洞；发现手写属性错误的3个漏洞及1个新漏洞。

Conclusion: CHARGE能有效自动生成硬件安全属性，减少人工依赖，并发现新漏洞，优于部分手写属性，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [120] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 针对医疗物联网边缘网关中突发流量导致时延恶化的问题，提出一种基于内核态的闭环自适应速率控制机制NANOEDGEGUARD，通过流量整形与迟滞恢复策略，在保证常规遥测的同时降低紧急报警的尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网网关需同时传输常规遥测数据与罕见但时间关键的报警信息，即使短暂的流量突发也会显著恶化最坏情况延迟，干扰紧急消息的及时送达。

Method: 设计内核级闭环控制器NANOEDGEGUARD，通过内核流量控制钩子实时观测每个数据源的流量强度，强制执行可审计的多级速率策略；采用迟滞机制实现快速动作与显式恢复，并记录策略转换日志以供审计，区别于静态防火墙规则或用户态控制循环。

Result: 在树莓派上部署MQTT代理和两个ESP32端点的实验设置中，相比用户态防火墙基线，该方法将报警消息的第99百分位往返时延降低13.3%，同时无强制控制时的RTT保持一致；相对于无强制控制，额外允许的突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，在边缘网关实施自适应的、可审计的速率控制能有效提升医疗物联网的弹性，并有望进一步扩展以支持未来边缘智能中的按需策略部署。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [121] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出“轨迹诱导退化”概念，指早期执行使后期任务更困难，并主张用“水平残差”（实际全长任务成功与基于短阶段基线预测的对数比）来判定长时程失败，需同配置、预设阶段与预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程基准仅显示任务越长失败越多，但未解释失败原因；阶段增加可能使普通错误累积，也可能因历史、工具输出或环境变化导致后期更困难。

Method: 提出水平残差作为评估指标：比较实际完整任务成功率与由短独立阶段构建的基线预测，二者对数比即残差；要求同一智能体配置，并预先指定阶段划分、检查点、信息流与预算。

Result: 明确水平残差仅能证明全长过程与基线不同，无法直接解释原因，需进一步设计针对性实验。

Conclusion: 真正识别“长时程失败”需用水平残差替代单纯成功率下降，并通过对照实验区分误差累积与轨迹诱导退化。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [122] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
*Jin-Seong Kim,Han-Ju Lee,Seok-Won Hong,Takeshi Takahashi,Chansu Han,Tomohiro Morikawa,Seok-Hwan Choi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出GradLock，一种训练时注入攻击方法，通过静态确定性索引和动态梯度锁定将敏感数据直接嵌入模型参数，实现近乎无损且即时的数据提取，并规避常见部署优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型逆向攻击受限于目标模型的泛化瓶颈，难以恢复高维数据中的具体身份，且训练时注入方法鲁棒性不足。

Method: 在受污染的供应链场景下，利用无状态确定性索引建立隔离数据存储库，并通过动态梯度锁定保护有效载荷在优化中不被破坏，从而实现从最终模型中直接提取像素级数据。

Result: 在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA上达到近无损重建（SSIM约1.0）和瞬时提取（<1秒），优于现有训练时注入方法，且对量化、剪枝和微调具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 用户部署实验中93.3%参与者未能检测恶意逻辑，凸显现代AI供应链安全性存在严重盲区，需加强防护。

Abstract: Existing model inversion (MI) attacks predominantly rely on post-training optimization to recover private data from model outputs. However, these methods are fundamentally constrained by the target model's generalization bottleneck, often yielding generic features rather than specific identities, particularly on high-dimensional datasets. In this paper, we introduce GradLock, a novel training-time injection attack that stealthily injects sensitive training data directly into the model parameters. Operating within a compromised supply chain context, GradLock leverages stateless deterministic indexing to establish isolated data vaults and employs dynamic gradient locking to prevent payload degradation during the optimization process. This mechanism allows the adversary to extract pixel-perfect data from the final model without retaining access to the training environment. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Imagenette, and CelebA demonstrate that GradLock achieves near-lossless reconstruction (SSIM ~ 1.0) and instant extraction (< 1.0s). Compared to existing training-time injection methods, our approach exhibits superior robustness against standard deployment optimizations, including quantization, pruning, and fine-tuning. Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [123] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双生成器学习的干净标签反遗忘后门框架，通过双层优化同时实现低预遗忘攻击率和高后遗忘激活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击在植入后立即生效易暴露，而基于机器遗忘激活的休眠后门可缓解该问题，但难以同时满足低预遗忘攻击率和强后遗忘激活，尤其在干净标签和现实遗忘请求下。

Method: 将后门建立与机器遗忘模拟为双层优化，交替学习样本特定触发器以建立潜在触发-目标关联，以及标签一致伪装样本提供可移除抑制；遗忘少量伪装样本后抑制解除，休眠后门激活。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上，相比代表性后门基线，该方法在多个遗忘算法下保持更低预遗忘攻击率并实现更强后遗忘激活。

Conclusion: 通过协调持久潜在关联与可移除抑制，可在干净标签和现实删除约束下实现可靠的休眠-激活转换。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [124] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出VAmoS Bench基准，通过模拟真实客服电话场景，端到端评估语音智能体在数据库操作和对话中的正确性，重点衡量'包含率'（无需人工介入的自主解决率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音智能体基准多关注组件质量（如词错误率、延迟），缺乏对完整智能体是否独立正确处理电话任务的评估，尤其是财务场景中的状态化操作与合规性。

Method: 构建包含100个场景的模拟任务：设置虚构银行信用卡客服智能体Riley，每个场景含私有目标的模拟呼叫者、PostgreSQL后端及可执行SQL的五个工具；约1/3场景施加对抗性压力；通过二进制断言和完整交互追溯（含对话、工具调用及数据变更）进行自动评估。

Result: 基准能捕获两种失败模式：谎称修改卡状态但数据库未更新，以及正确修改数据库却泄露敏感信息；支持在固定版本上持续评估新智能体，并允许后续扩展任务和场景。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench填补了语音智能体端到端效果评估空白，为复杂对话系统中状态管理、隐私保护及任务完成度提供可复现度量框架，并为动态排行榜和任务演进设计接口。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [125] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在双重用途任务中的安全困境，提出并证明了一个安全三难困境：有用能力、可靠安全与开放访问三者无法兼得，并论证可信凭证可作为一种补充机制以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的安全防护机制在回应请求前，无法预知回答的具体用途，导致在双重用途任务中，同一回答既可能服务于合法专业人员，也可能被攻击者利用；而攻击者可通过模仿正常请求及交互历史来绕过防护，这使得现有防护措施存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 作者将模型释放的能力与下游使用证据分离，针对可复制的证据，推导出在保留有用回答前提下攻击者获得协助的最坏情况下限，并由此提出安全三难困境；随后引入可信凭证作为补充，以添加难以复制的信息来预测真实下游使用情况，并进一步识别出消除该下限所需的条件。

Result: 研究表明，在开放访问条件下，无法同时保证模型的有用能力与可靠安全；而可信凭证机制能够有效降低攻击者协助下限，但需满足更强的条件才能完全消除该风险；双重用途评估、自适应攻击实验及部署中的可信访问项目均验证了相关条件的实际重要性。

Conclusion: 结论强调，安全三难困境揭示了现有防护的固有局限，可信凭证提供了一条可行的改善路径，但彻底解决双重用途风险需要严格的条件与机制配合，而非仅依赖单一技术手段。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [126] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次针对原生事件驱动SNN输入提出两类海绵攻击：逐样本梯度优化攻击使SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍，通用扰动攻击（XOR）增加1.09-1.24倍，且高概率保持分类正确，表明SNN能效优势可被利用为隐蔽能耗威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN的稀疏二进制事件通信带来能效优势，但这一特性可能被攻击者利用——通过增加脉冲活动和工作负载导致能耗上升，而正确率监控难以察觉，现有攻击局限于逐样本速率编码场景，缺乏对原生事件输入的通用攻击研究。

Method: 提出两种攻击：1）逐样本海绵攻击，通过梯度优化为每个输入生成对抗性脉冲序列；2）首个通用海绵攻击，离线计算固定二进制扰动，对后续所有输入执行XOR叠加，无需在线优化。

Result: 逐样本攻击在NMNIST、SHD和DVS Gesture数据集上对三个SNN模型使SynOps提升1.5-2.6倍，且至少98%样本类别不变；通用攻击提升1.09-1.24倍；映射Loihi-1能耗，每次推理额外消耗14μJ至13.24mJ。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动SNN对输入空间能效攻击存在实际脆弱性，可复用的通用扰动可在持续部署的边缘系统中累积为显著电池消耗，需关注此类隐蔽威胁。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [127] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次在脉冲神经网络上提出干净标签时间后门攻击，通过固定时间戳变换仅修改目标类训练流，不改标签，达到最高100%攻击成功率，并提出基于每步事件质量的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门攻击多依赖脏标签毒化，即触发样本被重标到攻击者选择的类，而干净标签场景更隐蔽且更贴近实际威胁。

Method: 对目标类训练流施加固定时间戳变换，保持每个像素、每个极性的事件计数完全不变，仅改变序列顺序，使时间聚合后干净与触发样本不可区分。

Result: 在三个神经形态数据集及卷积和Transformer架构上，最强配置攻击成功率（ASR）达1.00；时间轴折叠的防御完全失效，特征空间防御仅在部分设置中检测到；提出的模型无关检测器可检测所有测试的时间变换。

Conclusion: 证明了现有基于速率折叠的防御盲区，刻画了干净标签时间后门攻击的隐蔽边界，并展示了可检测性，为SNN安全研究填补空白。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [128] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SMT求解器的物联网访问控制策略信息流分析方法，通过构建信息流图识别设备间非预期通信漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网云平台（如AWS IoT Core）的访问控制机制配置不当会带来安全风险，但仅验证单个权限无法应对设备间信任级别不同或子系统隔离的场景，需检测跨设备非预期信息流动。

Method: 形式化建模AWS IoT Core组件，定义信息流图刻画访问控制策略允许的设备间通信，利用SMT求解器构造有限图表示以验证信息流可达性，并实现工具IOT:POKER。

Result: 在真实场景和多个实际策略上评估工具，证明其能有效发现潜在的信息流安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展应用于实际物联网访问控制策略审计，提升多设备环境下权限配置的安全性验证能力。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [129] [A dataset of rated conceptual arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27499)
*Emery Cooper,Caspar Oesterheld,Linh Chi Nguyen,Alexander Kastner,Ethan Perez*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含951条论证性评论、442篇立场文本和1,458条专家评分的数据集，用于评估大语言模型在概念性问题上的论证推理能力，并基准测试多种模型，发现性能与通用能力排名一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在可验证任务（如数学和编程）上进步迅速，但在概念性问题上（无唯一正确答案、依赖辩论论证）的能力尚不明确，而这类问题在哲学、AI安全、决策理论和社会选择中至关重要。

Method: 构建包含论证性评论和专家多维评分（中心性、力度、正确性、清晰度）的数据集，提出两种评分函数，并基准测试多种模型。

Result: 模型性能与通用能力排名基本一致，表明现有模型在概念性论证评估上尚未超越通用能力。

Conclusion: 概念性问题的论证评估仍具挑战，数据集和评分函数为后续研究提供基础，但模型表现受限于通用能力。

Abstract: Large language models have improved rapidly on tasks with verifiable answers, such as mathematics and programming. Much less is known about their ability to reason about what we call conceptual questions: questions for which no ground truth is realistically accessible and no widely accepted resolution methodology exists, but on which progress can still be made by debating arguments. Most philosophical questions are of this kind, as are central components of questions in AI safety, decision theory, and social choice. Our approach is based on the view that while bottom-line conclusions on such questions are hard to evaluate, individual contextualized arguments can be evaluated far more reliably. We therefore introduce a dataset of 951 argumentative critiques of 442 position texts, spanning topics from AI safety and decision theory to ethics and politics, with 1,458 ratings by six expert raters along dimensions including centrality, strength, correctness, and clarity. We propose two scoring functions and benchmark a range of models. Performance tracks general capability rankings.

</details>


### [130] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，证明自主多智能体系统(AMAS)中的推理时harness可被黑盒蒸馏提取，构成知识产权泄露风险，并提出基于欺骗的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注静态多智能体系统的IP泄露，而AMAS的harness在推理中动态涌现，其泄露风险未被探索；同时，harness构建成本高、价值大，亟需系统性安全分析。

Method: AHD采用两阶段黑盒提取：预蒸馏阶段从目标智能体响应中推断harness行为并构建初始harness；后蒸馏阶段迭代优化初始harness以匹配目标智能体的行为模式。

Result: 在多个真实AMAS和多种骨干LLM上的实验验证了AHD的有效性，揭示了显著的IP泄露风险；提出的欺骗式防御能降低提取效果，同时保持被保护智能体的效用。

Conclusion: AMAS的推理时harness存在可被蒸馏利用的新型安全威胁，AHD为该问题提供了首个量化评估框架，且防御策略可在效用与安全性间取得平衡。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [131] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl,Nelson Gardner-Challis,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Benjamin Allan-Rahill,Kaelan Yim,Damian Sójka,James Mann,Justin Olive*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文开发了AI扫描器，用于自动检测agentic基准测试中的四类有效性缺陷，并在多个广泛使用的基准上发现了质量问题，证明自动化审计的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工审计基准测试（如SWE-Bench-Verified）虽能揭示有效性问题，但难以规模化，且缺乏自动化方法可靠检测缺陷的证据。

Method: 设计AI扫描器检测四类问题（ground truth访问、工具失败、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义），制定分级标准，并在Inspect Evals基准的保留测试集上对照人工标注进行评估。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现多个已验证的质量问题，包括人工抽查难以发现的案例；但性能因基准、标准和模型而异，且存在漏检。

Conclusion: 研究作为概念验证，表明自动化转录分析可用于基准质量审计，但需解决标准化等开放挑战以提升扫描器的可靠性。

Abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [132] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于压缩相似度（NCD）的行为图框架，利用EVM交易轨迹构建符号化交易语法，无需资金关联即可发现Sybil钱包，并通过盲点协议过滤高信号合约，验证其抗时间漂移与对抗扰动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sybil检测依赖代币转账图或监督式机器学习，前者要求直接金融关联，后者视作闭集分类，难以应对策略演变与规避行为；需一种无需监督、不依赖资金链路且对漂移鲁棒的局部发现方法。

Method: 从EVM轨迹合成符号化交易语法，分通道刻画交易节奏、执行结构与功能意图；采用盲点协议筛选高信号合约；使用Gzip压缩的NCD构建行为图实现Sybil发现；对比监督基线、时间分割及合成伪装压力测试。

Result: 框架在无监督情况下可有效区分Sybil机器人、有机用户与套利机器人；高信号合约过滤提升发现能力；行为图在时间漂移与对抗扰动下保持稳健，超越监督基线。

Conclusion: 该工作提供一种免训练、漏报感知的Sybil候选发现原语，适合开放世界区块链审计的局部扩展，而非正式的开集识别系统。

Abstract: Sybil attackers are Blockchain actors that adopt the characteristics of regular users to exploit airdrops or influence governance. Current methods of Sybil actor detection include constructing graphs, which requires token transfers between examined wallets. Machine learning algorithms have been employed as well, but they treat the task as a closed-set classification problem, making them vulnerable to frequent changes in attack strategies or evasion tactics. We address the following questions: can compression-based similarity differentiate Sybil bots, organic users, and arbitrage bot wallets without direct financial links? What is the effect of high-signal contracts on the discovery of Sybils, and how robust are behavioral graphs under temporal drift and adversarial perturbations? Our approach synthesizes a symbolic Transaction Grammar from EVM (Ethereum Virtual Machine) traces, capturing separately transaction rhythm, execution structure, and functional intent. The high-signal contracts are filtered with our own protocol, called the Blind-Spot Protocol. Gzip-based NCD is used to construct a behavioral graph for Sybil discovery. We validate this framework against supervised machine learning baselines, a temporal split, and synthetic camouflage stress tests. Ultimately, we contribute a leakage-aware behavioral framework for Sybil candidate discovery. Its core NCD primitive requires no supervised training and can expand suspicious seed wallets without explicit funding links. We position the method as a training-free local discovery primitive for open-world blockchain audits, rather than as a formal open-set recognition system.

</details>


### [133] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击，提出指令增强与场景隐藏技术，构建基准AudioAgentSecurity，并设计基于声源分离与一致性分析的防御机制CADV，在11个智能体上平均攻击成功率69.10%，防御检测成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体通过连续音频交互执行任务时，音频输入不可避免包含环境噪声，这为恶意音频指令注入提供了隐蔽攻击面，但该威胁尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出指令增强与场景隐藏两种技术实现音频指令与用户语音的隐形叠加；构建涵盖8个真实场景和10种攻击模式的基准AudioAgentSecurity；设计基于声源分离和跨模态一致性分析的防御机制CADV。

Result: 在Gemini 3 Pro上平均攻击成功率达69.10%；CADV在多种攻击向量下检测成功率超过90%；真实环境实验验证了攻击的隐蔽性和有效性及防御的可靠性。

Conclusion: 音频提示注入对多模态智能体构成严重且隐蔽的威胁，但通过声学层面的源分离与一致性校验可有效防御，为构建安全的音频交互智能体提供了基准与实用方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [134] [The Convergence Behavior of Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27383)
*Yijiang Pang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次为普通向量形式Adam在重尾随机噪声下建立收敛保证，发现其迭代复杂度次优且依赖p，但在已知域半径时可达最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Adam变体在有限方差非凸优化中已知最优，但其在仅有p阶矩有界（p∈(1,2]）的重尾噪声下的行为缺乏理论理解，而该设定在现代深度学习中日益常见。

Method: 将在线到非凸转换框架推广至重尾鞅差噪声，并基于此对Adam进行带折扣的遗憾分析，无需限制性参数耦合。

Result: Adam收敛到(ρ,ε)-平稳点，但迭代复杂度次优且收敛速率依赖p；即便p=2时次优性仍存在。当已知域半径并用于控制在线学习器输出时，收敛速率提升至最优复杂度。

Conclusion: 揭示了Adam在重尾噪声下的鲁棒性与局限，表明其标准形式在一般设定下非最优，但利用域信息可弥补该缺陷。

Abstract: We establish the first convergence guarantees for the plain vector-form \emph{Adam} optimizer under heavy-tailed stochastic noise. While several Adam variants are known to achieve optimal iteration complexity in bounded-variance nonconvex optimization, little is understood about their behavior when stochastic gradients admit only a bounded $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1,2]$, a setting increasingly observed in modern deep learning. To address this gap, we generalize the recent online-to-nonconvex conversion framework to accommodate heavy-tailed martingale-difference noise. Building on this generalized framework, we develop a discounted regret analysis for Adam, without restrictive parameter coupling. Our results show that Adam converges to $(ρ,ε)$-stationary points under heavy-tailed noise. However, it exhibits a suboptimal iteration complexity and $p$-dependent convergence, a suboptimality that persists even in the bounded-variance case ($p=2$). When the domain radius is known and used to control the online-learner output, a standard setup in related literature, the convergence rate improves to match the optimal complexity. These findings provide new theoretical insight into the robustness and limitations of Adam in heavy-tailed regimes.

</details>


### [135] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了世界模型在具身AI中的安全威胁，提出生命周期分类法，涵盖从数据构建到部署的完整流程，并分析了攻击的双重性——既可作为安全防护工具，也可能因过度信任产生预测性安全错觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供预测核心，但其预测层引入了新的安全边界，威胁可从数据、传感器、提示或反馈传播至物理动作。现有研究多将世界模型视为孤立组件，缺乏对其全生命周期安全威胁的系统性分析。

Method: 提出生命周期分类法，从数据构建、表示学习、状态接地、想象模拟、轨迹评估、执行到长期适应（记忆与工具）全程追踪威胁；将常见攻击族（投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击）映射到世界模型的安全属性；设计评估协议和安全防御结构。

Result: 展示了攻击在破坏世界状态、学习动态、可供性估计或安全成本时的特殊含义，并揭示世界模型既可作为运行时安全防护，又可能在受损或过度信任时产生预测性安全错觉的双重性。

Conclusion: 提供了世界模型全生命周期的安全分类法，映射现有攻击到安全属性，提出评估协议和结构化防御框架（包括来源验证、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障），为构建安全可靠的具身AI世界模型奠定基础。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [136] [FunL2O: LLM-Guided Feature Function Design for Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27389)
*Bingheng Li,Junyang Cai,Yupeng Zhang,Bistra Dilkina,Jayant Kalagnanam,Dzung T. Phan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出FunL2O框架，用LLM驱动的程序进化自动设计L2O的特征函数，在连续和离散优化任务上超越手工特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有L2O方法依赖手工设计的特征函数，表示设计繁琐且跨领域固定，缺乏自动化机制，限制了性能提升。

Method: FunL2O采用FunSearch式循环：LLM生成可执行特征函数，固定评估流程重训原始L2O模型并测量下游优化性能，通过进化迭代优化特征。

Result: 在线性规划、二次规划、混合整数优化等任务上，四种LLM进化的特征均一致优于手工设计的表示。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的特征进化是一种通用且有效的自动化表示设计方法，可推广至多种L2O管线。

Abstract: Learning-to-optimize (L2O) methods accelerate repeated optimization by training models to predict solutions, warm starts, branching decisions, or other forms of solver guidance. A critical yet largely overlooked component of these pipelines is the feature function that maps problem instances to inputs for machine learning models. Existing L2O methods typically rely on hand-crafted features, making representation design manual and largely fixed across domains. We introduce FunL2O, the first unified framework for automating feature design through LLM-driven program evolution for L2O. In a FunSearch-style loop, an LLM proposes executable feature functions, while a fixed evaluation process retrains the original L2O model and measures downstream optimization performance. We evaluate FunL2O on linear and quadratic programming tasks involving solution prediction and warm-starting, as well as on mixed-integer optimization tasks using GNN-guided backdoor branching and Predict-and-Search. Across continuous and discrete optimization tasks and four LLMs, the evolved features consistently outperform hand-crafted representations. These results establish LLM-driven feature evolution as a general and effective approach to automating representation design in L2O.

</details>


### [137] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本章探讨证明助手在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译及密码学领域的应用，并强调其支撑认证的附加价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手不仅能验证设计与实现满足安全属性，还能为安全认证提供形式化依据。

Method: 通过系统梳理证明助手在上述四个安全方向中的典型用例与验证方法。

Result: 展示了证明助手在增强安全保证与辅助认证方面的实际效能。

Conclusion: 证明助手是连接安全验证与认证实践的重要工具。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


### [138] [Context-Informed Ship Trajectory Prediction via Conditional Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27418)
*Yuan Guan,Chandler Squires,Timothy Hu,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出条件信息器（Conditional Informer）架构，通过条件注意力机制将环境上下文编码为船舶动态的物理先验，并结合模态掩码训练策略，在AIS与ERA5数据上将预测精度提升15.4%，且将传感器缺失时的回退误差降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测模型将船舶视为孤立系统，仅依赖历史运动状态；多模态方法把环境变量当作对等特征，未编码环境对船舶动态的单向物理依赖关系，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器结构，设计条件注意力机制，使船舶状态通过交叉注意力显式查询环境上下文，编码‘天气调控但不由船舶动态生成’的物理先验；同时提出模态掩码训练策略，模拟传感器数据缺失场景，防止模型依赖捷径学习。

Result: 在AIS与ERA5数据上，相比运动学基线与拼接式基线，预测精度提升15.4%；模态掩码使传感器回退场景下的误差降低接近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 显式建模环境对船舶运动的单向条件依赖能显著提升长期轨迹预测能力，模态掩码有效增强模型在真实数据不完整场景下的鲁棒性，为海事自主导航提供更可靠的预测基础。

Abstract: Long-term ship trajectory prediction is a fundamental capability for maritime safety and autonomous navigation. While recent Transformer-based architectures have improved forecasting horizons, they predominantly rely on historical kinematic states, treating vessel motion as an isolated system. In reality, maritime navigation is profoundly modulated by extrinsic factors like weather and constrained by static vessel characteristics. Existing multimodal approaches fundamentally model the joint distribution over states and contexts, treating environmental variables as peer features rather than encoding the directional physical dependence of vessel dynamics on environmental conditions.
  In this work, we propose the Conditional Informer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture that formulates trajectory prediction as a conditional generation task. We employ a dedicated Conditional Attention mechanism where the vessel state explicitly queries environmental contexts through cross-attention, encoding the physical prior that weather modulates - but is not generated by - vessel dynamics. Furthermore, to address the intermittency of real-world data, we introduce a Modality Masking training strategy to prevent catastrophic degradation during sensor fallback. Extensive experiments on AIS and ERA5 data demonstrate that our approach outperforms kinematic and concatenation-based baselines by 15.4% in prediction accuracy when context is available. Crucially, Modality Masking prevents shortcut learning, reducing fallback error by nearly an order of magnitude compared to unconstrained models.

</details>


### [139] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文对三个商业图像审核API进行黑盒鲁棒性评估，发现简单图像变换即可绕过所有服务，且不同数据集和危害类别下鲁棒性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统审核分类器覆盖有限，新型多模态大模型API宣称更强大，但尚无系统性研究验证其鲁棒性是否真正提升。

Method: 对三个商业图像审核服务进行大规模黑盒测试，应用七种无梯度、模型无关的图像变换，跨多个提供方、数据集、危害类别及感知相似度约束评估。

Result: 所有三个服务均可被廉价变换绕过；固定变换如颜色反转和灰度化可导致不安全到安全的决策翻转；鲁棒性在数据集和类别间差异大，多模态内容和自伤类别尤其脆弱。

Conclusion: 直接用大模型API替换传统审核分类器不能提供可靠安全边界，需在现实变换下评估，并作为分层审核流水线组件而非独立过滤器部署。

Abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [140] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过结合提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励的强化学习，训练具备思维链推理的安全告警分诊分类器，并额外训练校准器以校正推理带来的置信度偏差，显著提升高置信度场景下的良性/恶意样本召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临告警疲劳，现有方法直接训练LLM输出分诊标签，但未训练其推理检测是否为真实威胁，导致性能受限。

Method: 在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上，采用自动化提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习训练CoT推理分类器；针对CoT导致的标签概率退化，另训练校准器读取完整推理轨迹以估计正确概率。

Result: 测试准确率82.6%，在高置信度工作点下良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%（对比直接标签LLM分类器）；未训练的置信度评判会使高置信度召回率降为零；微调30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。

Conclusion: 针对安全告警分诊，专门训练CoT推理与校准器比单纯依赖模型规模更有效，校准器是确保高置信度操作可靠性的必要组件。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [141] [Subtract or Replay? Exact Deletion from Language-Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27539)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 精确删除语言模型记忆取决于记忆表示方式：可寻址影响通过代数递减删除；共享循环状态中被后续写入变换的影响需从头重建。实验在多种规模模型上验证，并在临床记录上实现位级精确删除与修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有删除方法混淆了可寻址与纠缠的记忆表示，导致删除不精确或成本高昂。本文旨在区分两种记忆类型，并提供理论框架与实证验证，实现真正的精确删除。

Method: 采用两类模型：Gemma 3（替换全局注意力为支持向量记忆）与Kimi Linear混合模型。对支持向量记忆使用低秩恢复与递减/重拟合；对循环状态使用检查点回退重放；对delta规则分析后缀依赖性，并给出修正递减公式。

Result: 1B规模下递减与重拟合KL散度中位数5.4×10^-15，困惑度+2.0%；4B/12B下成本增至11.2%/44.3%。48B下delta规则12-49%贡献后缀依赖，回退重放在18,842 token临床记录上实现位级精确匹配，修正重放支持精确修改。

Conclusion: 精确删除是记忆表示的函数：可寻址记录直接递减，纠缠写入需重放。该区分在实际规模模型与真实数据上可行，为记忆编辑提供理论依据与实用算法。

Abstract: Exact deletion from persistent language-model memory depends on how that memory represents a record. Addressable influence can be removed by algebraic decrement; influence transformed by later writes inside shared recurrent state requires rebuilding from before the write. We test this distinction in two pretrained models against explicit record-omitted references. First, we replace Gemma 3's global-attention layers with support-vector memory. After low-rank recovery at 1B, decrement and retained-key refit agree at the next-token output to median KL $5.4\times10^{-15}$ over 31 support-token deletions, with $+2.0\%$ perplexity relative to a matched fine-tune. A masked-refit proxy is indistinguishable from the never-ingested floor under elicitation, relearning, sampling, and LiRA attacks. At 4B and 12B, certificate ordering persists but utility cost rises to $11.2\%$ and $44.3\%$. Second, in a 48B Kimi Linear hybrid, additive writes admit a fixed decrement and diagonal decay a corrected one, whereas the delta rule makes $12$--$49\%$ of a record's contribution suffix-dependent. Checkpointed rewind-and-replay deletes real clinical records at contexts up to 18,842 tokens, matching never-ingested logits and all recurrent states bit for bit within a deterministic MLX implementation; replaying a correction provides exact amendment. Exact deletion is therefore a property of memory representation: subtract addressable records and replay entangled writes.

</details>


### [142] [Compliance2LoRA: On-Demand Safety Alignment on Arbitrary Policy Subsets via Hypernetwork-Generated LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27594)
*Pankayaraj Pathmanathan,Furong Huang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自适应超网络的多策略对齐框架，通过生成LoRA权重实现单一大型推理模型按需适配不同安全策略子集，避免组合开销和长上下文计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型个性化需求增加，不同用户需遵守不同安全策略子集，单独训练每个策略子集的模型产生严重组合开销，上下文学习方法虽能缓解但引入长序列生成的计算成本。

Method: 构建统一自适应超网络，将安全策略作为可定制输入，通过LoRA适配器生成器为下游模型产出策略合规的LoRA权重，添加后使模型按指定策略生成响应。

Result: 在不同规模推理模型和多个评估数据集上，该方法支持单模型按需策略调整，同时保持任务性能不下降。

Conclusion: 自适应超网络对齐在大型推理模型中具有有效性和实用性，为多策略合规提供可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training alignment in large reasoning models (LRMs) has significantly improved their adaptability to diverse safety compliance settings. However, as LRMs personalization for downstream users takes center stage, the demand for varying levels of policy compliance grows as different user-specific LRMs must adhere to distinct subsets of safety policies. Training a separate LRM for each policy subset introduces severe combinatorial overhead. While in context learning methods overcome this combinatorial overhead, they introduce additional computational challenges associated with long context generation. To address this challenge, we propose \ours, a unified adaptive hypernetwork-based framework for multi-policy compliance. In our framework, safety policies serve as customizable inputs to a LoRA adapter generator, which learns to produce policy compliant LoRA weights for downstream LRM. When added to the LRM these weights enable the generation of responses compliant with the specified policy subsets. In this work, we demonstrate that training such a hypernetwork enables on-demand policy adjustments on a single LRM without sacrificing task performance across reasoning models of different sized and different evaluation datasets. This highlights the effectiveness and practicality of adaptive hypernetwork based alignment in LRMs.

</details>


### [143] [Real-Time Hard Peak Age-of-Information Safety with No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27626)
*Wentao Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出OCO-PAoI-Hard算法，将峰值信息年龄硬截止约束转化为仿射半空间约束，通过单次欧几里得投影实现零违规调度，并给出静态/动态遗憾界和匹配的极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（Whittle指数、深度强化学习、长期约束在线优化）仅在随机信道或软约束下工作，无法保证对抗性系数下每个时隙的峰值AoI硬截止。

Method: 将分数化峰值AoI截止等价为仿射半空间约束，采用严格因果的提案-屏蔽-更新循环，每时隙一次投影保证可行性，梯度步骤保持无遗憾，虚拟队列退化为事后证书。

Result: 在四传感器对抗性流体陷阱信道上，十次随机种子均实现零建模状态截止违规；四个基线违规率介于1.65%至64.0%之间；经验归一化遗憾低于理论包络线。

Conclusion: 理论分析确立O(sqrt(T))遗憾上界、匹配极小极大下界、针对执行噪声的边际安全变体及截止诱导竞争比，证明硬实时AoI调度可转化为约束在线凸优化问题并高效求解。

Abstract: Safety-critical IoT systems such as industrial closed-loop control, V2X coordination, and remote teleoperation require every sensor's peak Age of Information (peak AoI, also abbreviated PAoI) to stay below a hard per-slot deadline, not merely an average bound. Existing approaches meet this requirement only under restrictive assumptions: stochastic channels for Whittle-index AoI, simulator rollouts for deep reinforcement learning, or sublinear cumulative violation for long-term constrained online convex optimization. Under adversarial coefficients, OCO-PAoI-Hard guarantees zero per-slot violation of the modeled AoI state under one-step viability and O(sqrt(T)) regret against any static safe comparator; packet-level safety requires stronger service assumptions. Our key observation is that the fractional peak-AoI deadline collapses exactly to an affine half-space constraint on the resource-allocation vector, turning hard real-time scheduling into time-varying constrained online convex optimization over a polyhedral safe set. A strictly causal proposal-shield-update loop enforces feasibility through one Euclidean projection per slot, the gradient step preserves no-regret behavior, and the classical virtual queue is reduced to an a-posteriori certificate. We establish closed-form static and dynamic regret bounds, a matching Omega(sqrt(T)) minimax lower bound, a margin-safe variant against execution noise, and a deadline-induced competitive ratio. On a four-sensor adversarial fluid-model trap channel, OCO-PAoI-Hard attains zero modeled-state deadline violations across all ten seeds, while four representative baselines miss between 1.65 percent and 64.0 percent of slots, and the empirical normalized regret stays below the theoretical envelope across two orders of magnitude in T.

</details>


### [144] [First-order Constrained Trilevel Optimization Over Distributed Networks for Robust Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27632)
*Yang Jiao,Kaixuan Jiao,Kai Yang,Nadjib Aitsaadi,Ilhem Fajjari,Renwei,Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 针对分布式边缘网络中数据量大、隐私敏感及鲁棒性需求，提出首个分布式鲁棒核心集选择方法F²CTO，将问题建模为带层级约束的三层优化，并证明其以O(ε^{-3/2})速率收敛，实验验证在持续学习中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT产生海量数据，全量训练成本高，核心集选择是关键；同时局部数据隐私敏感且模型需鲁棒，但分布式鲁棒核心集选择尚未被探索。

Method: 将分布式鲁棒核心集选择建模为三层优化问题，提出F²CTO算法，结合层级复合值函数重构与分布式交替投影梯度方法，统一处理核心集、鲁棒优化与分布式学习的层次依赖。

Result: 理论证明达到ε-平稳点的非渐近收敛率为O(ε^{-3/2})；实验在可靠持续学习任务上验证了有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: F²CTO是首个面向分布式鲁棒核心集选择及带层级约束三层优化的方法，填补该领域空白，为隐私敏感分布式学习提供可行框架。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of the Internet of Things (IoT), massive amounts of data are generated across distributed edge networks. Training models on full data incurs significant computational overhead and storage bottlenecks, rendering coreset selection a critical paradigm. Furthermore, given the privacy-sensitive nature of local data and the escalating demand for model robustness in real-world deployments, developing an effective distributed optimization framework for robust coreset selection is vital, yet remains largely unexplored. To this end, this work first characterizes the hierarchical dependencies among coreset selection, robust optimization, and distributed learning, and formulates the distributed robust coreset selection as a trilevel optimization problem with level-wise constraints. Furthermore, to effectively solve the trilevel problem in a distributed manner, the \underline{F}ederated \underline{F}irst-order \underline{C}onstrained \underline{T}rilevel \underline{O}ptimization (F$^2$CTO) is proposed, which synergistically integrates a hierarchical composite value-function reformulation and a distributed alternating projected gradient algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, F$^2$CTO is the first method developed for distributed robust coreset selection, as well as the first distributed optimization approach for trilevel optimization problems with level-wise constraints. Additionally, we prove that the proposed method achieves a non-asymptotic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3/2})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary point. Extensive empirical evaluations on reliable continual learning demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed F$^2$CTO.

</details>


### [145] [Crossing the Margin Cliff: Toward Relearn-Robust LLM Unlearning via Margin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27836)
*Xiangyu Yin,Jiaxu Liu,Zhen Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究发现大语言模型遗忘在面对再学习攻击时普遍脆弱，并揭示了‘边际悬崖’这一规律，提出边际校准（MC）插件方法，显著提升遗忘鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型遗忘方法在再学习攻击下表现脆弱，仅用少量遗忘样本微调即可显著恢复遗忘知识，现有方法缺乏对此问题的理论理解和有效改进。

Method: 通过分析优化几何，证明边际悬崖源于保留耦合和令牌饱和损失；提出MC插件，结合非饱和边际铰链和KL探针，恢复遗忘侧压力，并给出攻击预算上界。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE-News和Phi-3.5等多个基准上，MC以固定配置赢得全部14项遗忘聚合指标和所有再学习单元，攻击后ROUGE-L从0.41降至0.18，降低了13/14的成员推理AUC，主要代价是保留侧效用下降。

Conclusion: MC方法有效且通用，无需保留训练参考即可部署，显著提升模型遗忘的鲁棒性，但需权衡保留侧效用。该研究为理解遗忘失败机制和设计鲁棒算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning is consistently fragile under relearn attacks. On TOFU, fine-tuning on twenty forget examples substantially recovers held-out forget-set ROUGE for every method we evaluate, and we trace this fragility to optimization geometry. The per-token answer margin of fourteen post-hoc methods spanning gradient, preference, and distillation families converges into a narrow band above the retain reference in 41 of 42 method--size cells, a regularity we call the margin cliff. We prove that this cliff follows whenever the retain coupling holds the diagnostic log-odds of forget content above a floor, a condition that token-saturating losses induce at stationarity and that we verify directly on 34 of 42 cells. Margin Calibration (\textsc{MC}) is a plug-in polish adding a non-saturating margin hinge anchored at the reference's per-token margin plus a KL probe on a disjoint instruction corpus, restoring forget-side pressure where the native loss saturates. Under a stated gradient-dominance condition, whose on-trajectory gradient signature we measure by instrumenting the polish, its stationary set lies on the cliff-crossing side, yielding an attack-budget upper bound on the relearn margin lift. Across TOFU (three Llama-3 sizes, three forget tiers), MUSE-News on Llama-2-7B-hf, and a Phi-3.5 panel, a single frozen configuration wins all 14 head-to-head forget aggregates and all populated relearn cells (panel-mean post-attack ROUGE-L $0.41$ to $0.18$) and lowers raw membership AUC on 13/14, with reduced retain-side utility as the main cost. A deployment variant matches these gains without a retain-trained reference.

</details>


### [146] [Looped Transformers with Source-Centered State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27656)
*Bum Jun Kim,Kohei Hayashi,Shunsuke Kamiya,Masanori Koyama,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出源中心状态演化（SCSE）方法，通过使锚点成为一步固定点并置零零偏差强制偏置，在循环Transformer中实现精确的输入条件保持与参考保持的循环计算，显著提升多个语言建模基准上的质量前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过共享同一模块在循环深度上重复计算，以固定参数数增加有效深度，但共享模块需管理整个轨迹的不同隐藏状态；且在加法注入的循环Transformer中，输入条件信号每一步都重新引入，使基于输入条件参考的共享转换仍可能移动隐藏状态，导致输入参考点不稳定。

Method: SCSE保留输入依赖通过学习的锚点和初始偏差，允许非零偏差驱动循环计算同时将零偏差映射为零，并通过零偏差掩码保证精确的锚点不变性。锚点通过构造成为一步固定点，零偏差强制偏置由锚点自身产生的下一偏差决定并在SCSE中置零，而非零偏差保持激活以支持状态依赖的循环计算。

Result: 在WikiText-2、WikiText-103、直接网页语料预训练、留出网页文本迁移和LAMBADA补全任务上，SCSE改进了受控的循环质量前沿；消融实验表明学习锚点和锚点坐标偏差循环是主要贡献因素，训练模型案例研究将锚点响应诊断与观察到的循环运动联系起来。

Conclusion: 零偏差强制偏置是设计自由度，其任务效应可能有害、中性或有益，SCSE通过置零该偏置以实现精确锚点不变性，从而在性能和稳定性上取得一致改进，验证了该方法在实际任务中的有效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Looped Transformers create a useful train- and test-time compute axis by reusing the same Transformer block over recurrent depth, increasing effective depth at a fixed parameter count. However, that shared block must then govern an entire trajectory of varying hidden states over trained and extrapolated depths. Furthermore, in additive-injection looped Transformers, an input-conditioned signal is reintroduced at every recurrent step, so applying the shared transition at an input-conditioned reference can still move the hidden state. In this paper, we propose Source-Centered State Evolution (SCSE), which is designed to reconcile input conditioning with reference-preserving shared recurrence. Specifically, SCSE retains input dependence through its learned anchor and initial deviation, allows nonzero deviations to drive recurrent computation while mapping zero deviation to zero, and guarantees exact anchor invariance through its zero-deviation mask. The designated anchor is thereby a one-step fixed point by construction. The zero-deviation forcing bias is the next deviation produced from the anchor itself and vanishes in SCSE, while nonzero deviations remain active and support state-dependent recurrent computation. Our theory shows that the zero-deviation forcing bias is a design degree of freedom whose task effect can be harmful, neutral, or beneficial; SCSE resolves this choice in favor of exact anchor invariance by setting the bias to zero. Across WikiText-2, WikiText-103, direct web-corpus pretraining, held-out web-text transfer, and LAMBADA completion, SCSE improves the controlled recurrent quality frontier. Ablation studies identify the learned anchor and the anchor-coordinate deviation recurrence as the primary contributors to the gain, and a trained-model case study grounds the anchor-response diagnostic in observed recurrent motion.

</details>


### [147] [VESTIGE: A Knowledge-Guided Masking Strategy for Corruption-Aware Fine-Tuning of Genomic Transformers, Validated on Ancient DNA Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27712)
*Angshuman Chakravertty,Rahul Maheshwari*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出VESTIGE，一种无需参数、即插即用的MLM数据整理器替代方案，按经验测得的逐位置损伤图谱重新分配掩码概率，在古DNA重建任务上显著优于标准MLM，且领域无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLM微调对所有位置使用统一掩码概率，假设重建难度与位置无关。当损伤具有位置特异性和集中性时，该假设失效，模型在峰值损伤位点性能甚至不如频率匹配的随机预测器。

Method: VESTIGE用mapDamage2测得的古DNA脱氨损伤梯度（C-to-T/G-to-A）重新缩放掩码分布，保持平均C/G掩码率为15%与标准MLM一致，仅改变空间分布；在DNABERT-2上对猛犸象CDS语料（两个样本、七个基因）进行对比实验，固定模型、数据、种子和超参数。

Result: 在6种末端区宽度和626对窗口下，VESTIGE全面领先（Delta +4.18至+10.35个百分点，p<10^-8），验证集交叉熵降低13%（3.274 vs 3.757）；在损伤放大10-30倍时，ESMFold重建TM-score均>0.95；1D CNN生物安全分类器AUC=0.935，98.2%重建窗口通过审查。

Conclusion: VESTIGE验证了知识引导的掩码分布可显著提升受损序列重建性能，且原则可推广至FFPE、亚硫酸盐、宏基因组或纳米孔等其他可测位置/上下文损伤场景，为智能系统提供通用训练范式。

Abstract: Standard masked-language-model fine-tuning applies a uniform masking probability across every token position, assuming reconstruction difficulty is position-agnostic. When the degradation process is characterised and concentrated at predictable positions, this assumption fails: at peak damage sites the model can underperform a frequency-matched random predictor. We introduce VESTIGE, a parameter-free, drop-in replacement for the standard MLM collator that aligns the masking distribution with an empirically measured per-position corruption profile. We apply it to ancient DNA (aDNA) reconstruction, where cytosine deamination produces a position-dependent C-to-T / G-to-A gradient quantified per-position by mapDamage2. Rescaling so the mean C/G masking rate equals 15% - identical to standard MLM - isolates spatial redistribution as the sole variable, with model, data, seed, and hyperparameters held fixed across both DNABERT-2 runs on a mammoth CDS corpus (two specimens, seven genes). Across six terminal-zone widths and 626 paired windows, VESTIGE leads standard MLM at every width (Delta = +4.18 to +10.35 pp, all p < 10^-8), cuts validation cross-entropy by 13% (3.274 vs. 3.757), and yields ESMFold reconstructions with TM-score > 0.95 across all six reconstructions (three genes) even under damage amplified 10-30x beyond authentic PMD rates. A 1D CNN biosecurity classifier returns AUC = 0.935 and clears 98.2% of reconstructed windows, the 1.76% remainder attributable to reference-genome features, not reconstruction artefacts. The principle is domain-agnostic: any measurable position- or context-specific corruption profile - FFPE, bisulfite, metagenomic, or nanopore - substitutes directly for the PMD array, making VESTIGE a knowledge-guided training routine for intelligent systems operating on degraded or noisy sequence inputs.

</details>


### [148] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tora Bodin,Rohan Menon,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文在统一的幅度控制协议下，系统比较了五种针对视觉语言模型越狱的推理时防御方向，发现没有单一方法在所有模型上同时最优，且防御方向具有强架构特异性，需按语言解码器家族分别校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时防御方法常从残差流中减去校准方向，但缺乏在多个模型和层上的一致比较，且未考虑干预幅度、随机对照和跨架构可迁移性，导致结论可能受实验设置偏差影响。

Method: 在15个模型-层单元（覆盖四个架构家族）中，以幅度匹配协议比较五种候选方向：均值图像条件偏移、CMRM式拒答方向、ShiftDC式攻击特定残差、提示忽略图像指令和随机对照；同时进行方向特异性检验、跨架构迁移测试，并分析文本与多模态拒答几何关系。

Result: 图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先且效用损失最低；提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上最优；攻击特定残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上最优。CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在全部15个单元中正对齐（均值0.35，显著性p≈3e-5），但图像条件方向仅在13/15单元中特异，且跨架构不可迁移。

Conclusion: 不同拒答方向恢复部分重叠的几何结构，但基于方向的防御必须针对每个语言解码器家族独立校准，不存在通用最优方向；建议在部署时按架构选择或融合策略。

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [149] [Improving the Robustness/Accuracy Tradeoff Against Adversarial Attacks Using Information Bottleneck Distillation Through Dual Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27737)
*Vincent Ryusuke Takahashi,Yoshinari Takeishi,Jun'ichi Takeuchi,Kave Salamatian*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种双教师蒸馏框架，在IBD基础上引入仅用干净样本训练的教师，通过跨层注意力矩阵融合干净与鲁棒特征，在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下提升干净样本精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IBD方法虽提升对抗鲁棒性，但会显著降低干净样本分类精度，需在两者间取得更好平衡。

Method: 在IBD基础上增加干净教师模型，与鲁棒教师共同通过跨层注意力矩阵向学生蒸馏特征，实现双教师知识迁移。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100上，干净样本精度较IBD提升，对抗样本精度保持相近，与B-MTARD等先进方法在干净-鲁棒调和均值上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 双教师注意力蒸馏能有效平衡干净精度与鲁棒性，且训练设置对注意力模块影响显著，为后续优化提供方向。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved remarkable success in classical machine learning problems. However, they are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Countermeasures proposed in the literature, notably Information Bottleneck Distillation (IBD) introduced by Kuang et al., degrade the classification accuracy on clean inputs while improving the robustness to adversarial inputs. In this work, we extend the IBD framework by introducing an extra teacher model (clean teacher) trained with only clean inputs, into the distillation process from a robust teacher model trained by adversarial training. The features of both clean and robust teachers are transferred to the student through a cross-layer attention matrix. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets show that the proposed method improves classification accuracy on clean samples compared to the original IBD, while maintaining similar accuracy on adversarial samples. Furthermore, our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art approaches, including the recent dual-teacher distillation framework B-MTARD, particularly in terms of the harmonic mean between clean and robust accuracy. We also analyze the impact of different training settings that have different influences on the attention module.

</details>
